### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

One mole of an ideal gas is taken along the process in which <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><msup><mi>V</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></math> = constant. The graph shown represents the variation of molar heat capacity of such a gas with respect to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>. The values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>'</mo></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>'</mo></math>. respectively, are given by

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/Question_No._66.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 150px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> <mtext>, </mtext> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math> <mrow>  <mi>P</mi><msup>   <mi>V</mi>   <mi>x</mi>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mtext>constant</mtext></mrow></math> 

<math> <mrow>  <mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><msub>   <mi>C</mi>   <mi>V</mi>  </msub>  <mo>+</mo><mfrac>   <mi>R</mi>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

At <math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>∞</mo> <mtext>,  </mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

from <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>PV</mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>constnat</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒   </mo> <msup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>/</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>V</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>another constant</mtext> </mrow> </math>

so at <math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>∞</mo> <mtext>,  </mtext> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

hence <math> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

and then <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

at x = 0, P = constant and C = C'

hence <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>C</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

at x = x', C = 0, so the process is adiabatic, hence

    <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Diagram shows the variation in the internal energy U with the volume V of 2.0 moles of an ideal gas in cyclic process abcda. The temperatures of the gas at b and c are 500 K and 300 K, respectively. Calculate the heat absorbed by the gas during the process.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0030796_Question.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 200px; height: 181px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** 400 R ln 2
- **(B)** 500 R ln 2
- **(C)** 700 R ln 2
- **(D)** 800 R ln 2

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 400 R ln 2**

**Detailed Solution:**

In the process a to b and c to d

as <math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>, therefore <math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math> or T = constant

                  <math> <mrow> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <munderover> <mo>&#8747;</mo> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mtext>i</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> </msub> </munderover> <mtext>PdV</mtext> </mrow> </math>

We have,

                <math> <mrow> <mtext>PV</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>nRT</mtext> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>nRT</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <munderover> <mo>&#8747;</mo> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mtext>i</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> </msub> </munderover> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>nRT</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>RV</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                  <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>nRT</mtext> <msubsup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>ln V</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mtext>i</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> </msub> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>nRT</mtext> <mo>ln</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mtext>i</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

          <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>ab</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>nRT</mtext> <mtext>b</mtext> </msub> <mo>ln</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>ln</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>R ln 2</mo> </mrow> </math>

and   <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>cd</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>nRT</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mo>ln</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>ln</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>R ln 2</mo> </mrow> </math>

There is no volume change from b to c and from d to a, so

              <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>bc</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>da</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

The work done in the complete cycle

       <math> <mrow> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>ab</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>bc</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>cd</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>da</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn><mo>R ln 2</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn><mo>R ln 2</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

           <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>R ln 2</mo> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An earthen pitcher loses <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of water per minute due to evaporation. If the water equivalent of pitcher is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> and the pitcher contains <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> of water, calculate the time required for the water in the pitcher to cool to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>28</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> from its original temperature of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>. Neglect radiation effects. Latent heat of vapourization of water in this range of temperature is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>580</mn><mo> </mo><mi>cal</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and specific heat of water is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kcal</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mo>°</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>38</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>min</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>min</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>34</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>min</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>41</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>min</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>34</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>min</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As water equivalent of pitcher is 0.5 kg, i..e., pitcher is equivalent to 0.5 kg of water, heat to be extracted from the system of water and pitcher for decreasing its temperature from 30 to 28<sup>o</sup>C is

       <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

           <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>9.5</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>0.5</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>kg </mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>k cal/kg</mo> <msup> <mtext>C</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext> </mrow> </math>

           <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>kcal</mo> </mrow> </math>

And heat extracted from the pitcher through evaporation in t minutes

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mL</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>dm</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>dt</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>t</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>L</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>min</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>t</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>cal</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

               <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>t cal</mtext> </mrow> </math>

According to given problem <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>,  i.e., <math> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>t</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

                                          t = 34.5 min

</details>

### Q72. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Diagram shows the variation of internal energy <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>U</mi><mo>)</mo></math> with the pressure <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>)</mo></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo> </mo><mi>mole</mi></math> gas in cyclic process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>abcda</mi></math>. The temperature of gas at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>500</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math>, respectively. Calculate the heat absorbed by the gas during the process.


                <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0030772_Question.jpg" style="width: 225px; height: 208px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>440</mn><mo> </mo><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>400</mn><mo> </mo><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>430</mn><mo> </mo><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>414</mn><mo> </mo><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>400</mn><mo> </mo><mi>R</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Change in internal energy for cyclic process <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>U</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

For process <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> <mtext>,&#160;&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

     <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>V</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>n</mtext> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>&#160;R</mtext> </mrow> </math>

For process <math> <mrow> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>c</mtext> <mtext>,&#160;&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>W</mtext><mrow><mtext>b</mtext><mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>nRTln</mi><mfenced><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>R</mtext><mfenced><mn>300</mn></mfenced><mtext>ln</mtext><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

For process <math> <mrow> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>d</mtext> <mtext>,&#160;&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>W</mtext><mrow><mtext>c</mtext><mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo><mtext>d</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>&#916;</mo><mtext>V</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext>nR</mtext><mo>&#916;</mo><mtext>T=</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn><mtext>R</mtext></math>

For process <math> <mrow> <mtext>d</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>,&#160;&#160;</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>constant</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

     <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>W</mtext><mrow><mtext>d</mtext><mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo><mtext>a</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>nRTln</mi><mfenced><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>R</mtext><mfenced><mrow><mn>5</mn><mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mtext>ln</mtext><mn>&#160;2</mn></math>

Net work,

    <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>b</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>b</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>c</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>c</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>d</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>d</mtext> <mo>&#160;&#8594;&#160;</mo> <mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </math>

      <math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>&#160;R ln 2</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>dQ</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>dU</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>dW</mtext> </mrow> </math>, first law of thermodynamics

 <math> <mrow> <mtext>dQ</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>&#160;R ln 2</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q73. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math> moles of an ideal diatomic gas is enclosed in a cylinder at temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math>. suppose on supplying heat to the gas, its temperature remains constant but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> moles get dissociated into atoms. Heat supplied to the gas is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>Zero</mtext> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>n</mi><mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since the gas is enclosed in a vessel, therefore, during heating process, volume of the gas remains constant. Hence, no work is done by the gas. It means heat supplied to the gas is used to increase its internal energy only.

Initial internal energy of the gas is <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>U</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>N</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Since n moles get dissociated into atoms, therefore, after heating, vessel contains <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>N</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>n</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>  moles of diatomic gas and 2n moles of a mono-atomic gas. Hence the internal energy for the gas, after heating, will be equal to <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>U</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>N</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>n</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>n</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>NRT</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>nRT</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Hence, the heat supplied = increase in internal energy

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>U</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>U</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>nRT</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q74. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An ideal gas <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>γ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> undergoes a thermodynamic process in which the temperature and pressure of the gas are related as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> = constant. The molar heat capacity of the gas during the process is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>3</mtext><mi>R</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>T</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi>P</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo></math> constant

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math><mi>P</mi><msup><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> = constant</p><p>For a polytropic process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><msup><mi>V</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></math> = constant, the molar heat capacity is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Substituting the values we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>R</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q75. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> of ice at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>20°C</mn></math> is mixed with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> of water at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20°C</mn></math>. The water content of the final mixture is (Latent heat of ice <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>80</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kcal</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>kg</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, the specific heat of water <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kcal</mi><msup><mi>kg</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>°</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and specific heat of ice <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mtext>0.5</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kcal</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mi>kg</mi><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>°</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi>kg</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kg</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi>kg</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi>kg</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kg</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> are the mass, specific heat and temperature difference for water respectively. Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are the corresponding quantities for ice. Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> is the latent heat of fusion.</p><p>We have, the heat lost by water <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>ms&#952;</mi></math>, heat gained by ice <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>MS&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ML</mi></math>.

From the principle of calorimetry, we have, the heat lost by water=heat gained by ice.</p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>ms&#952;</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>MS&#952;</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>ML</mi></math>.</p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>20</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>80</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math> of ice melts to form water.

Hence, final amount of water <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math>.

</details>

---

### Q76. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An ideal gas undergoes the cyclic process <math> <mrow> <mtext>abca</mtext></mrow> </math>as shown in the figure. The internal energy change of the gas along the path <math> <mrow> <mtext>c</mtext><mtext>a</mtext></mrow> </math>  is <math> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>180</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math>. The gas absorb <math> <mrow> <mn>250</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math> of heat along the path <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext><mtext>b</mtext></mrow> </math>  and also absorbs <math> <mrow> <mn>60</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math>  of heat along the path <math> <mrow> <mtext>bc</mtext></mrow> </math>. Find the work done by the gas along the path <math> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext><mtext>b</mtext><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-05-28_at_7.35.59_PM.jpeg" style="width: 200px; height: 190px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mn>130</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>140</mn><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>120</mn><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mn>100</mn><mo>&#x00A0;</mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mn>130</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Change in internal energy for a cyclic process is zero. Thus,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi>abc</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi>ca</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi>abc</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mi>ca</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>180</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;W</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#916;U</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>250</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>&#916;W</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>180</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#916;W</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>130</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q77. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Carbon monoxide is carried around a closed cyclic process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math>, in which <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi><mi>c</mi></math> is an isothermal process, as shown in the diagram. The gas absorbs <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7000</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math> of heat as its temperature is increased from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1000</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> in going from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>b</mi></math>. The quantity of heat ejected by the gas during the process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0026842_Question.jpg" style="width: 151px; height: 152px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>500</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9000</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9800</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9800</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>ab</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mo>+</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <msub> <mtext>&#160;C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>               ...(i)

For the process <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mi>a</mi></math>:

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>a</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>K</mo> </mrow> </math>,  <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>b</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>K</mo> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <msub> <mtext>&#160;C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <msub> <mtext>&#160;C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

             <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>               ...(ii)

For carbon monoxide:

         <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>a</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>K</mo> </mrow> </math>,  <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>h</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>K</mo> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <msub> <mtext>&#160;C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <msub> <mtext>&#160;C</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

             <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

For carbon monoxide:

             <math> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>C</mtext> <mtext>v</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&#947;</mi> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>               ...(iii)

Hence, from Eq. (i)

<math> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math> or  <math> <mrow> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mtext>R</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>Q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>ca</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>J</mo> </mrow> </math>

Negative sign shows that heat is ejected.

</details>

### Q78. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Determine the work done by an ideal gas undergoing a cyclic process from <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>→</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>→</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>→</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>→</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>. Given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>5</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mi>Pa</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>5</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mi>Pa</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>P</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>5</mn></msup><mo> </mo><mi>Pa</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-08-20_at_2.08.03_PM.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 220px; height: 177px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>740</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 90px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>750</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>730</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>745</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>750</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From figure

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>0</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>V</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                 <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>

Now, work done

<math> <mrow> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>J</mo> </mrow> </math>

</details>
